- **unfold-modeladmin**: Deep guide to unfold.admin.ModelAdmin — @display badges/headers, actions routing, sections (expandable rows), datasets (changelists inside changeform), sortable changelist, queryset optimization (select_related/prefetch/annotate, no N+1). Use when building or customizing a model's changelist/changeform, adding badges or computed columns, when the admin makes too many queries, or when deciding what logic belongs in ModelAdmin vs a service layer. - **unfold-navigation**: Configures admin navigation — SIDEBAR groups with icons/badges/collapsible sections/permissions, SITE_DROPDOWN, SITE_VIEWS, command palette (cmd+K) with search_models/search_callback, and user avatar/badge via model properties. - **unfold-performance**: Diagnoses and fixes slow admin pages — N+1 elimination (select_related/prefetch_related/annotate, the "500 queries" procedure), InfinitePaginator for huge tables (skips COUNT), search_fields indexing (trigram), filter cost, dashboard query budgets, and caching patterns. Use when an admin page is slow, when query counts explode with list_display methods, when a table has millions of rows, or when adding performance regression tests. - **unfold-production**: Production architecture for admin codebases — ModelAdmin as thin HTTP/permissions layer over a service layer, transactions for multi-step mutations, background jobs for long operations, audit logging, idempotency guards, environment/deployment checklist (collectstatic, Tailwind build, ENVIRONMENT label, session hardening). Use when admin features touch money/state/permissions, when designing service layers, or when preparing an Unfold admin for production. - **unfold-security**: Treats the admin as a high-privilege surface — per-surface authorization matrix (model perms, get_queryset data boundary, action permissions, custom page permission_required, nav/tab callbacks being hide-only, command palette callback filtering, dashboard scoping), information disclosure (secrets in list_display, search leakage, DEBUG), uploads, and audit expectations. Use when writing any action/custom view/dashboard, when reviewing admin permissions, or when admin data must stay tenant/role-scoped.
